  h256 baseline). Error bars are the paired bootstrap CI against C0 mapped onto
  the hit scale; the grey band is the split's measured resolution
  ($\pm\num{0.0124}$ recall@10 $=$ $\pm\num{17.7}$ hits,
  Figure~\ref{fig:nexttrack-power}), and every arm sits inside it. Four h211
  towers (B4) and one h512 GRU (C1) return the same \num{186} hits, paired
  $\Delta$ $\num{0.000000}$. \textbf{Middle:} the two arms that earned a walk
  TRADE at the primary cell both flip at the deployed cell --- the drift gain
  that bought the TRADE decays to a straddle while \texttt{vibe} goes below zero.
  Grey markers are point estimates whose CI straddles (the source report quotes
  bounds only for intervals clearing zero). \textbf{Right:} the campaign's one
  positive result, and it is not about topology --- at byte-identical parameters
  (\num{789696} both, \texttt{model.pt} \num{3162277} bytes both) dropout
  \num{0.1} beats dropout \num{0.0}, CI above zero.}
  \label{fig:nexttrack-tower-bank}
\end{figure}

\emph{Interpretation.} \textbf{The dose hypothesis is refuted, and it fails in
the direction opposite to the prediction.} Adding fixed-reference
\texttt{cummean} towers did not buy more of what $N=2$ bought; it monotonically
destroyed the walk. Ranked by $\Delta$\texttt{vibe} at the primary cell the
ordering is B4/B2 (all-\texttt{latent}, ${\approx}0$) $>$ B3 $>$ V3 (one
\texttt{cummean}, $-\num{0.0094}$) $>$ V2 ($-\num{0.0144}$, CI below zero) $>$
V1 ($-\num{0.0174}$, CI below zero), and the same ordering holds on S1, where the
three \texttt{cummean}-bearing arms occupy the \emph{bottom three} places
(\num{162}, \num{166}, \num{170} hits) and are the only arms whose
$\Delta$\,music@10 point estimates fall past $-\num{0.005}$. The irony is worth
stating plainly: \textbf{the tower added in order to hold a mood is the tower
that made the walk's mood measure worse} --- the only two arms whose
\texttt{vibe} is refuted outright are the two that carry the most
\texttt{cummean}. Whatever the deployed $N=2$ \texttt{latent}/\texttt{cummean}
engine is doing, ``more \texttt{cummean} is more mood-holding'' is not it, and
the structural reading that motivated the design does not survive being dosed.

\textbf{The sub-hypothesis is confirmed and extends cleanly from $N=2$ to
$N=4$.} B2 (\num{170}), B3 (\num{172}) and B4 (\num{186}) all straddle C0 and
show no trend distinguishable from width noise. This is the same mechanism the
dual-tower scan found (Section~\ref{sec:dualtower}) --- the raw \texttt{latent}
view subsumes its siblings --- now measured at three tower counts instead of two,
which is what closing an axis by dose rather than by a single point requires.

\textbf{The one positive finding is a by-product of the null, and it lands on the
deployed engine.} D2 and R2 differ in \emph{one} hyperparameter: parameters are
byte-identical (\num{789696} both, \texttt{model.pt} \num{3162277} bytes both),
only \texttt{dropout} moves, and $\Delta$recall@10 is $+\num{0.004892}$
$[+\num{0.000699},+\num{0.009783}]$, \textbf{CI above zero} --- dropout
\num{0.1} \emph{beats} dropout \num{0.0} (\num{177} against \num{170} hits).
Read carefully this does not overturn the record's three-times-confirmed ``f0
beats f1'' result; it does something more awkward. Dropout enters
\texttt{seq\_dualgru} only through a pre-MLP, an inter-layer gap or the fusion
MLP, and at \texttt{fusion\_layers}${}=0$ with one layer and no pre-encoder
\emph{all three are absent} --- the f0 path constructs \textbf{zero} dropout
modules. So every f0 arm on record, \textbf{including the deployed showcase
engine} (Section~\ref{sec:walk-limits}), trained under-regularized and was
leaving ${\approx}+\num{0.005}$ recall on the table; and the three recorded
f0-vs-f1 gaps ($+\num{0.016}$ / $+\num{0.020}$ / $+\num{0.0266}$) were measured
against an f1 that \emph{had} regularization its f0 comparator lacked, so they
are not like-for-like and the true topology advantage of f0 is if anything
\emph{larger} than recorded. Wherever those three figures are quoted as clean
topology measurements they should be re-stated as not like-regularized. The
cheapest actionable follow-up on the whole board falls out of this: retrain the
deployed engine at \texttt{dropout}${}=\num{0.1}$ and re-read both surfaces ---
a free improvement, not an architecture bet.

\textbf{The validation-loss dissociation gets a fourth confirmation, now as a
rank correlation} (Figure~\ref{fig:nexttrack-bank-valloss}). Across the ten arms,
Spearman(best validation InfoNCE, hits) $=-\num{0.2954}$, $p=\num{0.407}$ --- no
usable predictive power, and if anything the wrong sign. The sharp instances are
brutal: B4 and B2 differ by \num{0.0003} in validation loss and by \num{16}
hits; V1 holds the fourth-best validation loss in the batch and the \emph{worst}
hit count; C0 holds the \emph{worst} validation loss and beats six of the eight
bank arms; and R2 reaches a better validation loss than D2 while retrieving seven
fewer hits, which makes the dissociation and the dropout finding two faces of one
coin. The decision rule never read validation loss, and this is why.

\begin{figure}[ht]
  \centering
  \includegraphics[width=0.8\textwidth]{nexttrack_bank_valloss}
  \caption{The training objective does not rank retrieval --- fourth
  confirmation, and its sharpest instance yet. Ten arms of the tower-bank batch:
  best validation InfoNCE against hits of \num{1431}. Spearman
  $=-\num{0.2954}$, $p=\num{0.407}$. B4 and B2 are \num{0.0003} apart in
  validation loss and \num{16} hits apart; C0, the worst fit in the batch, beats
  six of the eight banks. Every arm early-stopped (epoch \num{14} seven times,
  then \num{21} and \num{24} twice), so no arm's recall is a lower bound imposed
  by the epoch cap.}
  \label{fig:nexttrack-bank-valloss}
\end{figure}

\textbf{Both walk TRADEs were cell-local, which is a methodological result in
its own right.} B2 and V3 each earned a real $\Delta$drift above zero at
$a\num{0.4}$/$s\num{0.3}$ with vibe held, and both flipped to a vibe below zero
at $a\num{0.8}$/$s\num{0.5}$ with the drift gain gone. Had the design not
pre-registered the secondary-cell requirement, this campaign would have reported
two positive walk results and saved two definitions. \textbf{The secondary-cell
clause is the clause that did the work here}, and it should stay mandatory in
every future walk design. The pre-registered stride probe points the same way: at
$a\num{0.4}$ over $s\in\{\num{0.2},\num{0.3},\num{0.4}\}$ all three
\texttt{cummean} arms peak at $s\num{0.3}$, the cell they were already measured
in --- the third campaign in a row in which re-tuning the stride rescues nothing
--- and at $s\num{0.2}$ their $\Delta$\texttt{vibe} turns
positive-straddling only by taking visibly shorter steps
($\Delta$\texttt{stride\_err} above zero for all three) and shedding genre
variety ($-\num{1.90}$ / $-\num{1.78}$ / $-\num{1.00}$, all below zero). That is
precisely the trade the walk win clause exists to reject.

\textbf{The failure signature is the opposite of the preceding architecture
campaign's, and that is diagnostic.} No arm here was refuted by a floor: the
recall floor and the relevance floor both pass for all ten runs, and eight of the
nine non-anchor arms sit at \emph{higher} artist concentration than the anchor
(\numrange{0.3547}{0.3799} against \num{0.3559}), i.e.\ the arms did not
de-eager. The floors were therefore not the operative mechanism --- these arms
are not degenerate, they are simply \textbf{flat}. That is a different failure
mode from the immediately preceding depth-in-series campaign
(Section~\ref{sec:stagestack}), in which all five arms fell \emph{through} the
recall floor, and it is why this axis closes as a null rather than as a harm.

\paragraph{The scope of the closure, precisely.} What closes is
``$N\ge3$ parallel bare-GRU tower banks at \texttt{fusion\_layers}${}=0$'', and
only that, to the standard Section~\ref{sec:stagestack} set for a closure on this
corpus: both surfaces,
walk instrument verified. It does \textbf{not} re-close the dual-tower family,
which stays reopened (Section~\ref{sec:walk-reversal}) and remains the deployed
engine. Nothing was promoted as champion, no definition was saved, and no
dataset was built; the eight measurement arms were promoted for measurement only,
each carrying the verbatim note ``promoted to enable walk evaluation; not a crown
claim'', and the resulting flooding of the best-models group (to \num{15}
entries) was disclosed up front and handed to the curation agent. Zero of the
three pre-authorized contingency runs fired, and the epoch trace says why: the
dropout-confound extension needed R2 to early-stop later than D2 and both stopped
at epoch \num{14}; the epoch-cap contingency needed an arm to reach the \num{40}
epoch cap and every arm early-stopped; the width-bracket contingency needed C1 to
lose, and it did not.

\paragraph{One limitation is inherited rather than introduced.} This campaign's
premise --- the dose mechanism --- was read off a finding that has \emph{no
campaign report of its own}: the walk-surface reopening of the dual-tower family
(Section~\ref{sec:walk-reversal}) exists only as a \texttt{PROJECT-FACTS.md}
roll-up, a git commit, and the transcription in this document. The
harness-reproduction gate re-derived all ten of that reopening's recorded means
exactly and re-derived its deltas, so the \emph{measurement} is reproducible and
the instrument has not moved; what is missing is the primary write-up, and it
should be backfilled from the commit and the facts entries.
\emph{Source: \texttt{2026-08-02-tower-bank-parallel-towers.md}.}

\section{Conclusions}
\label{sec:conclusions}
Twenty-one campaigns and one research memo (Jul--Aug 2026) on the next-track
retrieval task converge on a clear
story. (1) A session sequence model, even at its best (GRU-infonce), only
\emph{ties} the first-order Markov co-listening bar on Recall@10 and loses on
MRR: predicting a next-item latent and retrieving by one cosine hop is a harder
route to the exact next track than a bigram that directly memorizes adjacency.
(2) The training \emph{loss} is the dominant single-model lever (InfoNCE
$\approx 2.3\times$ cosine); network \emph{topology} --- depth, width, residual,
direction --- is a spent lever, and no architecture beats the bar. (3) The
breakthrough is \emph{rank fusion}: an R+M z-blend of the GRU and Markov legs
beats the bar by $\sim$$+61\%$ Recall@10 (three-seed confirmed), because the two
legs rank \emph{different} correct tracks. The win is about signal
\emph{diversity}, not model count --- adding a redundant neural leg does not
help, and a learned XGB stacker \emph{underperforms} the fixed z-blend, because
its pointwise training does not capture the ranking objective. (4) An
item-representation sweep found latent \emph{dimension}, not the compressor, to
be the lever: linear PCA beats the nonlinear autoencoder at every dim $\ge 64$
(the acoustic-preservation hypothesis is refuted), the curve peaks at PCA-192 and
turns over by 256, and PCA-128 is the confirmed single-model base. (5) On the
rich PCA space the one genuinely additive new signal --- \emph{content-kNN},
which only \emph{tied} the champion on the AE-64 space --- becomes a win: a
three-leg R+M+C z-blend on PCA-192 is the current best-on-record row at Recall@10
\num{0.1859}, MRR \num{0.1025} (paired $\Delta$ over the prior champion
$+\num{0.0133}$, CI above zero, confirmed across five reproductions). (6) Two
honest caveats bound the crown: the reproduction confirm had zero spread because
\texttt{seq-nexttrack} is deterministic on this CPU (so it verified determinism,
not seed-robustness), and the paired-$\Delta$ lower bound clears zero by only
$+\num{0.0007}$ on a single split --- hardening it needs a second leak-free test
split. (7) Every ranking predictor here is train-only and off-server, so the
crown is a best-on-record \emph{row}, not a promoted, app-exportable model; the
prior champion R+M on AE-64 remains the productization anchor, and registering a
three-leg blend with a \texttt{predict} subcommand is the standing top follow-up.
(8) A subsequent literature-fit campaign then \emph{superseded} the crown with the
first genuinely-additive new signal since content: a supervised \emph{learned
content projection} (\Rp+M+\Cp), an InfoNCE metric map of the PCA-192 item space
fit toward next-track adjacency in which both the GRU and content legs retrieve, at
Recall@10 \num{0.2117}, MRR \num{0.1220} (paired $\Delta$ over R+M+C $+\num{0.0259}$,
CI$>$0). It resolves both R+M+C caveats --- it is seed-robust across three seeds and
cold-reaching (its content leg scores cold targets as well as warm) --- and it was
\emph{registered, promoted and servable} (\texttt{blend-gru-markov-content-proj},
best-models rank 1), closing the productization gap; an objective / rank / $\tau$
scan confirms InfoNCE $\gg$ BPR and holds the default, and the crown then hardened
on a second disjoint 0.64-cold split (paired $\Delta$ $+\num{0.0426}$, CI$>$0, the
margin \emph{wider} on the colder split). Every borrowed literature-fit method
(session-kNN, rank-fusion / learned combiners, GRU-side loss tricks, Markov
smoothing) closed --- the only additive gain came from new signal, not smarter
fusion. (9) An item-vector representation exploration then confirmed \emph{why} the
projection is the champion: the equal-weighted PCA-192 geometry is a real handicap
for a raw model (six of eight whitened / acoustic-dropped / metric spaces lift the
frozen GRU, paired $\Delta$ CI$>$0), but the gain is fully \emph{absorbed} by the
learned projection (no space beats the champion on readout A) --- whitening and the
projection are the same fix, and the champion has already pulled it. (10) Finally, a
continuous ``sounds-alike'' metric, \emph{music@10}, was introduced and back-filled
across all 53 runs: it correlates with Recall@10 ($r=\num{0.609}$) but crowns a
different winner (the content-regressing \texttt{seq-nexttrack} lands the
musically-closest misses, best \num{0.482}), corroborating the exact-recall findings
without overturning them; it is display-only and the primary metric stays Recall@10. (11) Finally, a
measurement campaign added a display-only \emph{session-holisticness} suite
(artist\_adj@10 / mood\_coh@10 / ild@10) and two anti-eager levers. It found the
eagerness symptom \emph{tracks} Recall@10 inside the blend family --- the champion
is the most artist-eager model (artist\_adj@10 \num{0.610}) --- but the eagerness
is sourced from the \emph{Markov} bigram leg, not the RNN (single GRU / LSTM
artist\_adj@10 $\approx$ \num{0.345}), and the GRU and LSTM are statistically
tied on both recall and eagerness (the ``GRU wins because more eager'' hypothesis
is refuted). Of the two levers the eval-time MMR re-rank is the strong knob
($\lambda{=}\num{0.9}$ near-free: recall tie, ild@10 \num{0.406}$\to$\num{0.436};
$\lambda\approx\num{0.7}$ de-eagers for $\sim$2 recall points; music@10 even
rises as the list diversifies) while the training-time $\beta$ regularizer is weak
($\beta$ \numrange{0}{0.2} moves artist\_adj@10 only $-\num{0.0074}$); nothing
was promoted, and $\lambda\approx\num{0.9}$ is flagged for a future user
holisticness decision (Section~\ref{sec:holisticness}).
(12) Two final architecture campaigns then closed the last open architecture
axes, and both are nulls --- but nulls with mechanism. A jointly-trained
\emph{dual-tower} GRU, crossing the complete
$\{$\texttt{latent},\texttt{delta},\texttt{cummean}$\}^2$ view matrix against a
\SI{0.098}{\percent} parameter-matched single tower, fails to beat that tower on
any of eight arms (Section~\ref{sec:dualtower}). This is the \emph{fourth}
independent learned-combiner null and the one that matters most, because it
answers the standing rebuttal to the earlier three (``of course a bolted-on
combiner fails --- train the fusion end-to-end''): trained end-to-end at the
representation level, it still fails. The mechanism is that the raw
\texttt{latent} view already \emph{subsumes} the others --- a GRU over raw
latents integrates movement and running mean internally, and the one genuinely
complementary pair is \texttt{delta}$+$\texttt{cummean}, which beats both its
parents yet is hopeless in absolute terms. Two by-products are durable: the MLP
fusion head is actively \emph{harmful} (removing it wins in every regime
measured, three times over, Table~\ref{tab:fusion-depth}), and single-GRU width
is now closed on PCA-192 as it already was on AE-64. A learned per-step
\emph{pre-encoder} then fails harder (Section~\ref{sec:preencoder}): the
decisive single-tower ablation \emph{loses} outright ($-\num{0.0140}$, CI below
zero, \num{156} hits against \num{176}), the dose curve is flat to twice the
input dimension, and placement is irrelevant. Because a \emph{linear}
pre-encoder is provably expressivity-neutral here, the rectifier is the only
candidate cause --- and a counterfactual interpretability read confirmed it,
measuring the affine map neutral at ${\sim}0.8\sigma$ and the ReLU costing
${\sim}5.9\sigma$, with \texttt{frac\_exact\_zero} \num{0.5327} matching
\texttt{frac\_negative\_coords} \num{0.5327} to four digits: half-wave
rectification of a zero-centred latent deletes exactly its negative half. Across
five architecture nulls the pattern is now hard to dismiss --- on this
single-user, \SI{55}{\percent}-cold corpus, adding representation machinery
\emph{around} a single GRU over a good latent space does not help, while every
real gain came from improving the latent space itself or from the fixed z-blend.
(13) Finally, two facts about the \emph{instrument} rather than any model
reframe the whole architecture arc (Section~\ref{sec:power}). The nulls are
\textbf{power-limited}: at $n=\num{1431}$ the paired-bootstrap CI half-width is
\num{0.0124} on Recall@10 and ${\sim}\num{0.0018}$ on holisticness@10, while a
whole architecture family spans only \num{0.028} and \num{0.0031} --- so of
eight adjacent arms roughly two tiers are separable on recall and one on
holisticness, and resolving a \num{0.005} difference would need
${\sim}\num{8800}$ sessions. The honest reading is therefore that \emph{large
harms were detected reliably while moderate wins were never detectable at all},
which is a different claim from ``these architectures do not work'' and yields a
prioritization rule: further architecture arms are a poor use of runs on this
split whatever they return, because the axis's whole dynamic range is barely
twice the measurement error. And \emph{holisticness@10} exists in three
mutually non-comparable code generations (3-factor; 4-factor grounded;
grounded plus artist-concentration), whose differences dwarf every model
difference and under all of which the servable champion ranks \emph{last} ---
so this synthesis keeps its board denominated in Recall@10 and tags every
holisticness column with its generation (Table~\ref{tab:hgen}).
(14) Finally, a campaign about the \emph{surface} rather than the model finds
that the board above had been scoring the wrong one
(Section~\ref{sec:walk}). Everything else in this document is a one-shot top-10,
while the shipped product serves an autoregressive \emph{walk} --- and
\texttt{holisticness@10}'s \texttt{mood\_coh} anchors to the \emph{prefix}
centroid, which on a walk grows with the journey, so a run of individually legal
steps that ends in the opposite region of the space scores well at every step:
drift was invisible by construction, and the composite is $\num{5.5}\times$ more
sensitive to relevance than to eagerness besides. Calibrated against the user's
own \num{73632} consecutive transitions (median step cosine \num{0.261},
\SI{27.0}{\percent} negative), the engine the showcase shipped walks
$\num{2.5}\times$ too tight (median step \num{0.661}) --- ``flat despite lots of
artists'' was many small safe moves --- while the blend champion matches the real
stride but cannot hold a mood, because its InfoNCE projection encodes what
\emph{follows} rather than what \emph{sounds alike}. Three deterministic
retrieval-time controls fix the reachability: a seed anchor, a stride term
(deterministic on purpose --- sampling would break the permalink guarantee), and
a \emph{hard} per-artist cap, which succeeds exactly where the two soft
anti-eager levers failed because it cannot be outscored (on the pathological
playlist \num{59} of the top \num{60} candidates are one artist and the next
artist sits at rank \num{75}, against an MMR diversity budget worth
${\approx}\num{0.078}$ of normalized score; \texttt{eager\_beta} is mis-specified
on an absolute cosine when eagerness is a \emph{ranking} property). The cap
hardens on two disjoint splits and, unexpectedly, \emph{raises} \texttt{music@10}
at every setting: it trades literal for graded relevance. And on \num{40}
identical held-out sessions under paired bootstrap the \textbf{dual-tower family,
closed by three campaigns on recall grounds, is the winner on the stated
objective} --- \texttt{latent/cummean} at anchor \num{0.8} stride \num{0.5} takes
stride error $-\num{0.2545}$, genres $+\num{1.50}$ and drift $+\num{0.0351}$
with every CI clear of zero while \emph{holding} vibe, where the single GRU
($-\num{0.0409}$) and the champion ($-\num{0.1327}$) both pay vibe for the same
stride. The corroboration was already in the record as a \emph{negative}
(\texttt{seq\_dualgru.py:113}: the \texttt{cummean} tower ``added real linear
directions (CKA \num{0.458}) but zero next-item concepts tower A lacked'') ---
dead weight for next-track prediction is exactly session state for mood-holding,
though the stronger reading is refuted: \texttt{cummean} does \emph{not} hold a
vibe natively (\num{0.236} against \num{0.234}), it only hosts one once the
controls are applied. It is deployed as the showcase's default engine; and
\texttt{ground} straddles zero for every arm, so what is established is journey
\emph{shape}, not next-track truth, on one split, one seed and \num{40}
sessions, with nothing yet listened to and the primary metric unchanged.
(15) A closing architecture campaign then tested the reversal's own stated
mechanism --- ``holding a mood needs a fixed reference never overwritten by
recurrence'' --- as a \emph{dose}, and closed the axis on both surfaces
(Section~\ref{sec:towerbank}). A bank of $N$ parallel bare-GRU towers under one
linear head, matched at \num{3.00}$\times$ the h256 baseline and dosed from
$N=2$ to $N=4$, reaches no win on either surface: every arm \emph{ties} the
baseline on one-shot recall while \textbf{five of eight lose to a
parameter-matched single GRU} with the CI below zero, and the two arms that earn a
walk trade at the primary cell both flip to \texttt{vibe} below zero at the
deployed cell, so no definition was saved. The decisive datapoint is a zero: four
h211 towers (\num{1187700} params) and one h512 GRU (\num{1182912} params)
return the \emph{identical} \num{186} hits of \num{1431}, paired $\Delta$
\num{0.000000} --- \emph{topology and width are interchangeable at this budget},
which is also the cleanest demonstration on record of why the parameter-matched
control leg is mandatory: against the anchor alone, five losing arms would have
read as ties. The dose hypothesis is refuted \emph{ironically} --- the
\texttt{cummean} towers added in order to hold a mood are the worst arms in the
batch on both surfaces and the only two whose walk \texttt{vibe} is refuted
outright --- and the one CI-clear result in the whole batch is a by-product about
regularization, not topology: at byte-identical parameters dropout \num{0.1}
beats dropout \num{0.0} by $+\num{0.0049}$ recall@10. That is consequential
because the \texttt{fusion\_layers}${}=0$ path constructs \emph{zero} dropout
modules, so every f0 arm on record, \textbf{including the deployed showcase
engine}, trained under-regularized, the three recorded f0-vs-f1 gaps are not
like-regularized, and the cheapest improvement on the board is a single retrain.

(16) Three score-time campaigns then produced the arc's first genuine
\emph{holisticness} lever and its largest \emph{recall} gain, neither of them a
model change. The eval-time MMR $\lambda$ frontier (Section~\ref{sec:mmr}) is
confirmed natively on both anchors: every one of eight $\lambda<1$ arms lifts
holisticness@10 with the CI above zero, three single-GRU arms clear the prior
all-time bar \num{0.024872} at a recall tie (best \num{0.030355} at
$\lambda\num{0.7}$, pool~\num{50}), and the gain is legitimate rather than
composite-gaming because \emph{three} of the four crown factors --- diversity,
eagerness and \texttt{music@10} --- improve together. Its pre-registered mechanism
question resolves unanimously to the responsive hypothesis (8 of 8 arms,
$|\Delta\texttt{artist\_conc}|/|\Delta\texttt{artist\_adj}|$ between \num{1.59} and
\num{3.06}), so same-artist tracks \emph{are} near-neighbours in the content
metric, sonic MMR \emph{is} a de-eagering lever, and the explicit artist-penalty
sequel is cancelled. Two by-products are durable: the binding facet
\emph{switches} from concentration to adjacency as $\lambda$ falls, which locates
the end of the lever; and the shared-weights pairing is $\numrange{3}{33}\times$
sharper than the architecture-family noise floor (half-widths from
\num{0.000382}), so eval-time levers must budget their minimum detectable effect
from the pairing, not from Section~\ref{sec:power-stats}'s figure.
(17) The largest single recall gain in the whole arc is then \texttt{markov\_gate}
(Section~\ref{sec:markovgate}): make the Markov leg \emph{abstain} where the last
prefix item has zero train-bigram support --- \SI{55.6}{\percent} of queries --- and
Recall@10 rises to \num{0.23201} (\num{332} of \num{1431}), paired
$+\num{0.02027}$ $[+\num{0.01048}, +\num{0.03075}]$ canonically and
$+\num{0.01957}$ on a disjoint \SI{64}{\percent}-cold split, MRR up on both. The
defect is the blend's \emph{scale-invariant} normalizer, which re-inflates an
evidence-free artist/genre back-off to a full one-third vote; the fix has zero
learned parameters and nine lines. The bucket decomposition is the argument: the
whole effect sits in the zero-support bucket while both warm buckets are
\textbf{byte-for-byte identical} on three independent control/arm pairs, which
mechanically proves no global static leg weight could express it (dropping the leg
globally is a $-\num{0.0070}$ tie, the two buckets cancelling) and therefore
\emph{narrows} the four-times-confirmed learned-combiner pitfall to \emph{learned
score-level fusion} rather than per-query evidence gating. Unpredicted, it is also
a crown lever --- all four factors move the right way at once, and it is the first
lever that de-eagers the champion \emph{without} paying mood coherence, because it
removes a vote that had no business being cast rather than fighting the model's own
scores. Composed with MMR $\lambda\num{0.7}$ it is recall-neutral against the
champion ($+\num{0.00140}$, straddling) at holisticness \num{0.030980},
$\num{3.6}\times$ the champion's. Nothing was promoted: this is a recommendation,
and the one residual concern is that the zero-support \emph{boundary} was chosen
with test knowledge.
(18) Against those two positives, the training-time anti-eager lever is refuted
with a mechanism, and the refutation is expressive rather than dosimetric
(Section~\ref{sec:eagermargin}). The margins were calibrated from the
item$\to$item transition cosine (p75 \num{0.584}, p90 \num{0.830}) but the hinge
acts on prediction$\to$current, whose ceiling over training lies between
\num{0.5843} and \num{0.70} --- so the calibrated margins fall \emph{outside the
support of the penalized quantity} and four arms are provably inert, reproducing
the control's loss to \num{16} significant digits at every epoch at $\beta$ up to
the schema cap. Where the knob fires at margin zero it de-eagers spectacularly and
destroys relevance in the same motion, because an InfoNCE prediction is a hedged
near-average and eagerness is a \emph{ranking} property that an absolute cosine
cannot express. The prior ``weak knob'' reading is sharpened rather than retired:
the entire usable range at margin zero lies in an unexplored factor of \num{15}
between $\beta\num{0.2}$ and $\beta\num{3}$.
(19) Two campaigns about \emph{instruments} rather than models bracket these.
A purpose-built rollout metric (Section~\ref{sec:rollout}) --- fade per legible
axis, calibrated on \num{2135} real train sessions, with the anti-gaming term
\emph{inside} the metric rather than bolted on as an external recall floor ---
returns a sixth architecture null in the deployment regime, which is the strongest
form available because the standing rebuttal to the earlier five was that Recall@10
cannot see session quality. Its durable finding is that \emph{per-seed} variance
dominates model identity ($\pm\num{0.3}$ per seed against a model-level difference
of $+\num{0.004}$), so budget $\ge\num{40}$ seeds for any rollout comparison and
prefer serving an ensemble; and its three retracted positives are a catalogue of
how a generation metric goes wrong (a cross-split comparison read as architecture,
mood-extreme seed selection inflating effects ${\sim}\num{2.5}\times$, and an
anti-gaming term normalized by band width that scored a flatline \emph{above} a
real generator). A research memo (Section~\ref{sec:memo}) is reported at a lower
register --- nothing in it was run on the server, and its verified and provisional
numbers are kept partitioned --- but its convergent thesis, that every proposal
surviving adversarial screening is an \emph{evidence-conditioning} change rather
than a capacity change, is what points at (17), and its graveyard is cheaper to
reuse than to rediscover.
(20) Finally, a stage-stack depth campaign (Section~\ref{sec:stagestack}) closes
the \emph{serial} branch of the architecture axis to the two-surface standard the
tower-bank campaign then inherits. Five arrangements of per-step MLP and recurrent
stages, capacity-equalised at ${\sim}\num{1282000}$ parameters, all fall
\emph{through} the mandatory recall floor (\numrange{0.0706}{0.0860} against
\num{0.108}) and are refuted again on the walk at the matched cell, while a
$\num{3.24}\times$ capacity control ties on both surfaces --- so the damage is depth
itself, not capacity, not regularization (every deep arm trains \emph{longer} and
two reach a \emph{lower} validation loss) and not the MLP (a leading per-step MLP
is recall-neutral and holisticness-positive at depth 4, which \emph{narrows} the
$N=1$ pre-encoder result to the shallow case). Two facts outlive the null:
\emph{bracketing beats interleaving} at byte-identical capacity
($+\num{0.01537}$, CI above zero), and the training objective \textbf{dissociates}
from retrieval --- the arm with the best validation InfoNCE in the batch retrieves
\num{55} fewer hits than the control, so any architecture search selecting on
validation loss on this family will select the wrong arm.

\section{Source reports}
This synthesis consolidates twenty-two next-track source documents from
\texttt{experiments/} --- twenty-one campaign reports and one research memo, the
latter flagged as such because nothing in it was run on the server:
\begin{itemize}
  \item \texttt{2026-07-13-nexttrack-sequence-sweep.md} --- the founding
    next-track campaign: a GRU/LSTM sequence sweep against the first-order Markov
    bar; the best GRU-infonce ties on R@10 but loses on MRR
    (Section~\ref{sec:sweep}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-13-nexttrack-topology-scan.md} --- the depth / width /
    residual / bidirectional topology scan; architecture is a spent lever, all
    arms cap below the Markov bar (Section~\ref{sec:topology}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-14-nexttrack-blend-and-ensemble.md} --- the
    GRU$\times$Markov R+M z-blend beats the bar $+61\%$ R@10 (the first
    next-track champion); no richer ensemble improves on it and the learned XGB
    stacker underperforms (Section~\ref{sec:blend}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-14-nexttrack-graded-relevance-and-new-legs.md} ---
    first-class graded-relevance metrics (artist@k / genre@k / artist\_mrr) and
    new leak-free base learners; content-kNN is the only additive signal, tying
    the champion on AE-64 (Section~\ref{sec:graded}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-15-nexttrack-compression-representation-bakeoff.md} ---
    the item-representation compression bake-off (PCA vs autoencoder $\times$
    latent dim 32/64/128) under a frozen GRU readout; PCA-128 is the single-GRU
    winner but the win does not carry into the blend (Section~\ref{sec:compression}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-15b-nexttrack-compression-dim-extension.md} --- the
    PCA/AE dimension sweep extended to 192/256; the curve peaks at PCA-192 and
    turns over, so PCA-128 stays the Phase-2 base (Section~\ref{sec:dim}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-15c-nexttrack-phase2-model-sweep.md} --- the model-family
    sweep on the rich PCA spaces; the fixed z-blend beats the learned stacker,
    LSTM is the best single model, and R+M+C on PCA-192 is crowned the new
    best-on-record row (Section~\ref{sec:phase2}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-18-nexttrack-literature-fit-campaign.md} --- a
    bibliography-driven exploration; a supervised learned content projection
    (\Rp+M+\Cp) is the new best-on-record row (R@10 \num{0.2117}, paired $\Delta$
    over R+M+C $+\num{0.0259}$ CI$>$0, seed-robust, cold-reaching), and every
    borrowed method (session-kNN, rank fusion, learned combiner, Markov smoothing,
    GRU importance negatives) closes (Section~\ref{sec:projection}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-18b-nexttrack-projection-registration-and-scan.md} --- the
    projection reproduced on-server, registered $+$ promoted $+$ servable as
    \texttt{blend-gru-markov-content-proj} (best-models rank 1); an objective / rank
    / $\tau$ scan (InfoNCE $\gg$ BPR; default held) and second-split hardening on a
    disjoint 0.64-cold split (paired $\Delta$ $+\num{0.0426}$ CI$>$0)
    (Section~\ref{sec:projection-scan}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-18c-nexttrack-representation-exploration.md} --- an
    item-vector representation audit; the equal-weighted PCA-192 geometry handicaps
    the raw GRU (six of eight alt spaces lift it CI$>$0) but the gain is absorbed by
    the projection (no space beats the champion on readout A)
    (Section~\ref{sec:representation}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-20-musical-distance-metric.md} --- the continuous
    ``sounds-alike'' music@10 metric and its 53-run offline backfill; it correlates
    with Recall@10 ($r=\num{0.609}$) but crowns a different winner, the
    content-regressing \texttt{seq-nexttrack} (Section~\ref{sec:music}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-22-session-holisticness-and-antieager-levers.md} ---
    a display-only session-holisticness metric suite (artist\_adj@10 /
    mood\_coh@10 / ild@10) and two anti-eager levers (eval-time MMR
    \texttt{mmr\_lambda}, training-time \texttt{eager\_beta}); the champion is
    the most artist-eager model, the eagerness is sourced from the Markov leg,
    GRU\,$\approx$\,LSTM, and MMR $\lambda$ is the strong knob
    (Section~\ref{sec:holisticness}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-25-nexttrack-dual-tower-fusion-scan.md} --- the
    dual-tower view-pairing $\times$ fusion-depth scan against a
    \SI{0.098}{\percent} parameter-matched single tower; \textbf{REFUTED} (the
    fourth learned-combiner null, and the one that closes the ``train the fusion
    end-to-end'' rebuttal), with the raw-latent view shown to subsume
    \texttt{delta}/\texttt{cummean}, the MLP fusion head shown actively harmful,
    single-GRU width closed on PCA-192, and holisticness/recall shown
    anti-correlated across the batch (Section~\ref{sec:dualtower}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-26-nexttrack-pre-encoder-scan.md} --- the learned
    per-step pre-encoder scan (presence $\times$ placement $\times$ dose) with
    the decisive single-tower ablation; \textbf{REFUTED and actively harmful}
    ($-\num{0.0140}$ Recall@10, CI below zero), the rectifier identified
    analytically and then measured (affine map neutral at ${\sim}0.8\sigma$, ReLU
    ${\sim}5.9\sigma$, \texttt{frac\_exact\_zero} $=$
    \texttt{frac\_negative\_coords} $=$ \num{0.5327}), plus the measured power
    analysis and the three holisticness generations
    (Sections~\ref{sec:preencoder} and~\ref{sec:power}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-26-nexttrack-mmr-lambda-frontier.md} --- the native
    eval-time MMR $\lambda$ frontier on both anchors; \textbf{CONFIRMED} as the
    first genuine holisticness lever on record (three TIER-1 ``free'' single-GRU
    arms above the prior all-time bar at a recall tie, best $H$ \num{0.030355}),
    with the pre-registered mechanism question resolved unanimously to the
    responsive hypothesis (8 of 8 arms, ratio \numrange{1.59}{3.06}), the binding
    facet shown to switch as $\lambda$ falls, the candidate-pool axis closed as a
    fine-tuning knob, and the shared-weights pairing measured
    $\numrange{3}{33}\times$ sharper than the architecture-family noise floor
    (Section~\ref{sec:mmr}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-27-rollout-hold-metric.md} --- \texttt{hold@}$N$, a
    generation metric for the autoregressive rollout regime (per-axis fade
    calibrated on \num{2135} real train sessions, with the anti-gaming term inside
    the metric), plus five sweeps and \num{240} journeys; \textbf{REFUTED} as a
    model discriminator --- a sixth architecture null, obtained in the deployment
    regime --- with per-seed variance shown to dominate model identity and three
    retracted positives documented as a catalogue of generation-metric pitfalls
    (Section~\ref{sec:rollout}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-27b-nexttrack-model-topology-research-memo.md} ---
    \textbf{a research memo, not a campaign report}: nothing was run on the
    server, nothing promoted, nothing registered, and its numbers come from
    offline screens over frozen checkpoints. Consolidated at a lower register,
    with its own verified/provisional partition preserved and its test-set
    screening caveat stated; its reusable content is the convergent thesis that
    every surviving proposal is an \emph{evidence-conditioning} change rather than
    a capacity change, the mechanical explanation of the learned-combiner anomaly,
    and the graveyard (Section~\ref{sec:memo}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-27c-nexttrack-markov-gate-confirm.md} --- the native
    two-split confirm of \texttt{markov\_gate}; \textbf{CONFIRMED} and the largest
    single recall gain in the arc (R@10 \num{0.23201} $=$ \num{332} of \num{1431},
    paired $+\num{0.02027}$ canonically and $+\num{0.01957}$ on a disjoint
    \SI{64}{\percent}-cold split, MRR up on both), with the warm evidence buckets
    \emph{byte-for-byte identical}, an unpredicted crown gain in which all four
    factors move the right way at once, a first recall-neutral crown arm when
    composed with MMR, and the learned-combiner pitfall narrowed accordingly
    (Section~\ref{sec:markovgate}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-30-eager-margin-calibrated-refutation.md} --- the
    training-time eagerness regularizer at margins calibrated from the measured
    transition tail; \textbf{REFUTED across the whole axis, with a mechanism}: the
    margins were drawn from the item$\to$item cosine while the hinge acts on
    prediction$\to$current, whose ceiling sits between \num{0.5843} and \num{0.70},
    so four arms are provably inert (loss traces bit-identical at $\beta$ up to the
    schema cap) and the arms that fire destroy relevance before they help
    (Section~\ref{sec:eagermargin}).
  \item \texttt{2026-07-30c-artist-cap-measurement.md} --- the \emph{hard
    per-artist cap}, the first de-eagering lever that works, and it works because
    it cannot be outscored: \texttt{artist\_conc@10}
    \num{0.692}$\to$\num{0.123} with \texttt{music@10} \emph{rising} (CI above
    zero) at every setting, hardened on a second disjoint 0.64-cold split,
    together with the refutations of the two soft levers it replaces
    (Section~\ref{sec:walk-controls}). The walk-level material of
    Section~\ref{sec:walk} --- the surface mismatch, the four walk metrics, the
    anchor $\times$ stride frontier, the matched-sample head-to-head and the
    dual-tower reversal --- is transcribed from the \texttt{PROJECT-FACTS.md}
    roll-up of 2026-07-31b and from the four measurement tools it cites
    (\texttt{tools/walk\_eval.py}, \texttt{walk\_frontier.py},
    \texttt{walk\_dualgru.py}, \texttt{walk\_headtohead.py}), whose module
    docstrings are the authority for how each metric is computed; that material
    has no campaign report of its own yet.
  \item \texttt{2026-08-01-stage-stack-topology.md} --- the stage-stack
    topology campaign (arrangements of per-step MLP $\times$ recurrence at
    equalised capacity, via the new \texttt{seq-stack} predictor);
    \textbf{REFUTED on both surfaces} and the first closure on this corpus to that
    standard: all five 4-stage arms fall \emph{through} the recall floor and are
    refuted again on the walk, the $\num{3.24}\times$ capacity control ties on
    both, the user's rectifier-position hypothesis is refuted, bracketing beats
    interleaving at byte-identical capacity, and the best-fitting arm in the batch
    retrieves \num{55} fewer hits than the control
    (Section~\ref{sec:stagestack}).
  \item \texttt{2026-08-02-tower-bank-parallel-towers.md} --- the
    parallel-tower-bank dose campaign ($N$ bare-GRU towers under one linear head,
    via the new \texttt{seq-bank} predictor), \textbf{CLOSED on both surfaces}:
    no arm wins, five of eight lose to a parameter-matched single GRU, four h211
    towers and one h512 GRU return the identical \num{186} hits (paired $\Delta$
    \num{0.000000}), the \texttt{cummean} dose makes the walk strictly worse,
    both walk trades are cell-local, and the batch's one CI-clear result is that
    dropout \num{0.1} beats dropout \num{0.0} at byte-identical parameters ---
    which implies every \texttt{fusion\_layers}${}=0$ arm on record, the deployed
    engine included, trained under-regularized (Section~\ref{sec:towerbank}).
\end{itemize}
